# MedicalOS

**Open-source integration, governance and evidence platform for medical AI services.**

> MedicalOS integrates, governs and evidences medical AI services. It does not diagnose, does not replace a PACS, and is not itself a medical device.

| | |
|---|---|
| Specification version | **0.4.0** |
| Status | Development specification |
| Chapters | 19 |
| Normative requirements | 2456 |

---

## How to read this

Every requirement carries a stable ID of the form `MOS-<AREA>-<NNN>` and uses MUST / MUST NOT / SHOULD / SHOULD NOT / MAY in the RFC 2119 sense. Cross-references are requirement IDs, never section numbers, because IDs are stable across revisions and section numbers are not.

Each chapter owns a subject and defers to the owning requirement ID rather than restating it. **Chapter 12 is normative for database structure; the owning chapter is normative for behaviour, field names and enum values.**

| If you are… | Start with | Conformance profile |
|---|---|---|
| Building a medical AI service to run on MedicalOS | 2, 4, 7, 9, 17 | `service-publisher` |
| Deploying MedicalOS beside an existing PACS | 1, 3, 8, 9, 13 | `deploying-site` |
| Building an operator surface | 10, 19 | `platform-core` |
| Building or operating the platform itself | all | `platform-core` |

**Undefined scope is not permission.** A function in neither the in-scope table nor the non-goals list is undefined; raise it in chapter 16 rather than resolving it by assumption.
